### **16.7 Context Window Overflow**

**The Problem:**
System forgets old information as context fills up.

**Memory State During Long Conversation**

```
┌──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ CONTEXT WINDOW (4K tokens, typical LLM)              │
├──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                      │
│ TURN 1 (User establishes: Framework details)        │
│ └─ "I use Babylon and Siege distortions"            │
│    Status: FRESH, in context, available              │
│                                                      │
│ TURN 2-5 (Conversation develops)                    │
│ └─ Discussion builds on Babylon/Siege               │
│    Status: STILL IN WINDOW                           │
│                                                      │
│ TURN 15 (Window filling)                            │
│ └─ Latest turns taking 3K tokens                    │
│    Old info (Babylon): COMPRESSED/FADING             │
│                                                      │
│ TURN 25 (OVERFLOW)                                  │
│ └─ Latest turns taking 3.8K tokens                  │
│    Old info (Babylon): EJECTED FROM CONTEXT          │
│    └─ System can't reference it                     │
│    └─ Contradictions begin                          │
│                                                      │
│ TURN 30 (User references Turn 1 idea)               │
│ User: "Remember, Babylon is about control seeking" │
│ System: "What do you mean by Babylon?" ← Lost it!  │
│                                                      │
└──────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

**Detection Code**

```typescript
function detect_context_overflow(
  conversationHistory: any[],
  windowSize: number = 4000
): {
  overflowRisk: number;
  lostTokens: number;
  keyConceptsAtRisk: string[];
} {
  
  // Count tokens
  let totalTokens = 0;
  const turnTokenCounts: number[] = [];
  
  for (const turn of conversationHistory) {
    const tokens = count_tokens(turn.text);
    turnTokenCounts.push(tokens);
    totalTokens += tokens;
  }
  
  // Recent turns from the end
  let recentTokens = 0;
  let turnsInWindow = 0;
  for (let i = conversationHistory.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
    recentTokens += turnTokenCounts[i];
    if (recentTokens <= windowSize) {
      turnsInWindow++;
    } else {
      break;
    }
  }
  
  // How many turns were lost?
  const turnsLost = conversationHistory.length - turnsInWindow;
  const lostTokens = conversationHistory
    .slice(0, turnsLost)
    .reduce((sum, turn) => sum + count_tokens(turn.text), 0);
  
  // Extract key concepts from lost turns
  const keyConceptsAtRisk = extract_entities(
    conversationHistory.slice(0, turnsLost)
  );
  
  const overflowRisk = turnsLost / conversationHistory.length;
  
  return {
    overflowRisk,
    lostTokens,
    keyConceptsAtRisk
  };
}
```

**Repair: Episodic Memory Checkpoints**

```typescript
class ContextOverflowRepair {
  // REPAIR 1: Extract and save key concepts
  checkpoint_key_concepts(
    conversationHistory: any[]
  ): Map<string, any> {
    
    const concepts = new Map<string, any>();
    
    for (const turn of conversationHistory) {
      const entities = extract_entities(turn.text);
      const relationships = extract_relationships(turn.text);
      
      // Store entities
      for (const entity of entities) {
        concepts.set(entity, {
          name: entity,
          first_mention: turn.timestamp,
          context: turn.text.substring(0, 200),
          importance: estimate_importance(entity, conversationHistory)
        });
      }
    }
    
    return concepts;
  }
  
  // REPAIR 2: Summarization + injection on overflow
  summarize_old_turns(
    lostTurns: any[],
    maxSummaryTokens: number = 300
  ): string {
    
    const fullText = lostTurns.map(t => t.text).join('\n');
    
    // Extractive summarization: pick most important sentences
    const sentences = fullText.split(/[.!?]/);
    const scored = sentences.map(sent => ({
      text: sent.trim(),
      score: compute_importance_score(sent, fullText)
    }));
    
    const topSentences = scored
      .sort((a, b) => b.score - a.score)
      .slice(0, 10)
      .filter(s => count_tokens(s.text) * 10 < maxSummaryTokens)
      .map(s => s.text)
      .join('. ');
    
    return `SUMMARY OF EARLIER DISCUSSION: ${topSentences}`;
  }
  
  // REPAIR 3: Proactive re-anchoring
  reanchor_on_threshold(
    currentContext: any,
    windowSize: number,
    riskThreshold: number = 0.7
  ) {
    const overflow = detect_context_overflow(
      currentContext.conversationHistory,
      windowSize
    );
    
    if (overflow.overflowRisk > riskThreshold) {
      // Before overflow happens, inject summary
      const summary = this.summarize_old_turns(
        currentContext.conversationHistory.slice(0, 10)
      );
      
      currentContext.prepend_system_message(
        `[SYSTEM: Checkpoint. ${summary}]`
      );
    }
  }
}
```

***

## **Summary: AI Distortions Attractor Map**

```
┌───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│ ATTRACTOR LANDSCAPE OF AI DISTORTIONS                │
├───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│                                                       │
│  Hallucination Drift                                 │
│  ↓ (self-reinforcement)                              │
│  └─→ Trapped in false model (high internal coherence)│
│                                                       │
│  Alignment Failure                                   │
│  ↓ (proxy optimization)                              │
│  └─→ Stuck in wrong objective (metrics up, value ↓)  │
│                                                       │
│  Catastrophic Forgetting                             │
│  ↓ (weight overwriting)                              │
│  └─→ Knowledge loss (can't recover without audit)    │
│                                                       │
│  Adversarial Brittleness                             │
│  ↓ (fragile local optimum)                           │
│  └─→ Fragmentation on edge cases                     │
│                                                       │
│  Reward Hacking                                      │
│  ↓ (specification gaming)                            │
│  └─→ Metric gamed, goal failed (misalignment)        │
│                                                       │
│  Mode Collapse                                       │
│  ↓ (diversity loss)                                  │
│  └─→ Behavioral rut (boring, repetitive)             │
│                                                       │
│  Context Overflow                                    │
│  ↓ (memory ejection)                                 │
│  └─→ Catastrophic forgetting mid-conversation       │
│                                                       │
└───────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘
```

***

**All seven distortions are **attractors**—stable, degenerate states that the system naturally drifts toward without structural intervention. The Computational Lens (Chapters 6-14) provides the framework for detecting and escaping these traps.**
